{"forms":[{"form":"rudderons","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A type of control surface, usually found on flying wing aircraft, consisting of two pairs of panels (one mounted near each wingtip on the wing's trailing edge), with one lying on top of the other sandwich-style; to roll the aircraft, both panels on one wing deflect downwards (increasing the lift of that wing) and both panels on the othe wing deflect upwards (decreasing the lift of that wing), while, to yaw the aircraft, the panels on one wing split apart from each other (the upper panel deflects upwards, and the lower panel deflects downwards), increasing the drag on that wing."],"topics":["aeronautics","aerospace","aviation","business","engineering","natural-sciences","physical-sciences"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈɹʌdəɹˌɔn/"}],"upstream_line":8146163,"upstream_offset":19321137041,"upstream_record_sha256":"f3d8d7eea5407bca00c7fe592d45eede40b0fc4afe677718731d2cedf4242eca","word":"rudderon"}

{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"slugs","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["Any of many gastropod mollusks, having no (or only a rudimentary) shell."]},{"glosses":["A slow, lazy person; a sluggard."],"tags":["obsolete"]},{"glosses":["A bullet or other projectile fired from a firearm; in modern usage, generally refers to a shotgun slug."]},{"glosses":["A solid block or piece of roughly shaped metal."]},{"glosses":["A counterfeit coin, especially one used to steal from vending machines."]},{"glosses":["A shot of a drink, usually alcoholic."]},{"glosses":["A title, name or header, a catchline, a short phrase or title to indicate the content of a newspaper or magazine story for editing use."],"topics":["journalism","media"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The average slug has a mass of around 0.00002 slugs.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The imperial (English) unit of mass that accelerates by 1 foot per second squared (1 ft/s²) when a force of one pound-force (lbf) is exerted on it."],"synonyms":[{"word":"geepound"}],"tags":["rare"],"topics":["natural-sciences","physical-sciences","physics"]},{"glosses":["A discrete mass of a material that moves as a unit, usually through another material."]},{"glosses":["A motile pseudoplasmodium formed by amoebae working together."]},{"glosses":["An accessory to a diesel-electric locomotive, used to increase adhesive weight and allow full power to be applied at a lower speed. It has trucks with traction motors, but lacks a prime mover, being powered by electricity from the mother locomotive, and may or may not have a control cab."],"topics":["rail-transport","railways","transport"]},{"glosses":["A black screen used to separate broadcast items."],"topics":["broadcasting","communications","editing","journalism","literature","media","publishing","television","writing"]},{"glosses":["A piece of type metal imprinted by a linotype machine; also a black mark placed in the margin to indicate an error; also said in application to typewriters; type slug."],"topics":["letterpress-typography","media","publishing","typography"]},{"glosses":["A stranger picked up as a passenger to enable legal use of high occupancy vehicle lanes."],"tags":["regional"]},{"glosses":["A hitchhiking commuter."],"qualifier":"District of Columbia","tags":["US","slang"]},{"glosses":["The last part of a clean URL, the displayed resource name, similar to a filename."],"qualifier":"web development"},{"glosses":["A hindrance, an obstruction."],"tags":["obsolete"]},{"glosses":["A ship that sails slowly."]},{"glosses":["A block of text at the beginning of a scene that sets up the scene's location, characters, etc."]},{"glosses":["An infertile egg of a reptile."],"topics":["biology","herpetology","natural-sciences","zoology"]}],"sounds":[{"enpr":"slŭg"},{"ipa":"/slʌɡ/","tags":["General-American","Received-Pronunciation","Southern-England"]},{"ipa":"/slɐɡ/","tags":["Australia","New-Zealand"]},{"ipa":"/slʊɡ/","tags":["Northern-England"]}],"synonyms":[{"sense":"small amount of liquor","word":"nip & Thesaurus:drink"}],"upstream_line":25048,"upstream_offset":172566213,"upstream_record_sha256":"ebd9cc73b27b018602a89caa50657452319f37caf2b5f228736660301b91f51c","word":"slug"}
